About Us
關於我們



As a next-generation stock investing assistant, we provide equity       作為新一代股票投資助手，我們面向廣大個
investment tools, securities investment advisory, investor education    人投資者提供股票投資工具、證券投資顧
and other services for individual investors with product offerings      問、投資者教育等服務，相繼推出股道領
such as Stock Navigator, Super Investor, Decision Master,               航、超級投資家、決策大師、AI股票機、九
Enjoy-Stock Pad, Jiuyao Stocks and Star-tier Services.                  爻股及星級服務等產品。

Implementing our “technology + investment research” dual-driver         我們秉持「科技＋投研」雙輪驅動戰略，基於
strategy, and leveraging artificial intelligence and big data           人工智能與大數據技術研發九方靈犀等AI產
technologies, we have developed AI products such as FinSphere           品，實現行業領先的創新實踐與場景應用，
Agent, achieved industry-leading innovative practices and scenario      致力於成為客戶投資理財的終身伴侶。
applications, and stayed committed to becoming a lifelong partner
in investment and wealth management for our customers.

and wealth management

BUSINESS REVIEW AND OUTLOOK                                              業務回顧與展望
As a next-generation stock investing assistant, we provide equity        作為新一代股票投資助手，我們面向廣大個
investment tools, securities investment advisory, investor education     人投資者提供股票投資工具、證券投資顧
and other services for individual investors with product offerings       問、投資者教育等服務，相繼推出股道領
such as Stock Navigator, Super Investor, Decision Master,                航、超級投資家、決策大師、AI股票機、九
Enjoy-Stock Pad, Jiuyao Stocks and Star-tier Services.                   爻股及星級服務等產品。

Implementing our “technology + investment research” dual-driver          我們秉持「科技+投研」雙輪驅動戰略，基於
strategy, and leveraging artificial intelligence and big data            人工智能與大數據技術研發九方靈犀等AI產
technologies, we have developed AI products such as FinSphere            品，實現行業領先的創新實踐與場景應用，
Agent, achieved industry-leading innovative practices and scenario       致力於成為客戶投資理財的終身伴侶。
applications, and stayed committed to becoming a lifelong partner
in investment and wealth management for our customers.

Business Review                                                          業務回顧
We consistently adhere to the concepts of rational investment,           我們始終貫徹理性投資、價值投資和長期投
value investment and long-term investment, and insist on adopting        資理念，堅持以客戶為中心，深入推進「境
a customer-centric approach to further advance the strategy of           內多元產品佈局+海外業務探索」   ：AI與量化
“diversifying product layout domestically + exploring business           產品同步實現突破－以「九方AI靈犀」為核心
overseas”. Simultaneous breakthroughs were achieved in AI and            的多智能體服務體系全面落地，AI原生App
quantitative products – the multi-agent service system centered          「九方靈犀 claw」正式推出，星投量化平台

synergistic advancement across our multi-level product system. The       商，培育第二增長曲線，全球化佈局紮實推
enhancement of product capabilities driven by AI and quantitative        進。
technology is translating into sustained improvements in customer
repurchases and stickiness. Overseas, the Company completed
the strategic acquisitions of entities including Forthright Securities
and Forthright Capital in January 2026 and is advancing their
integration in an orderly manner. The Company has established
“dedicated investment advisory”, “in-depth investment research”
and “AI intelligent investment” as its core engines, building a new
generation of internet securities brokerage, cultivating a second
growth curve and steadily advancing its global expansion strategy.

Investment policies and objectives                                       投資政策及目標
The Group has implemented the Administrative Measures for                本集團已實施《理財投資管理辦法》（「投資政
Wealth Management and Investment (       《理財投資管理辦法》) (the                策」），旨在規範投資行為、提升自有資金營
“Investment Policy”), aiming to standardize investment practices,        運效率及實現資產保值增值。本集團在進行
improve the operational efficiency of own funds, and achieve asset       理財投資活動時嚴格遵守國家法律法規、適
preservation and appreciation. In conducting wealth management           用地方法律法規及相關監管要求，確保所有
and investment activities, the Group strictly complies with national     投資活動合法合規。透過科學的風險評估及
laws and regulations, applicable local laws and regulations, and         控制措施、跨投資類型及投資標的的分散配
relevant regulatory requirements to ensure that all investment           置與分層管控，本集團力求優先保障本公司
activities are lawful and compliant. Through scientific risk             資金安全、降低投資虧損風險、避免過度集
assessment and control measures, diversified allocation across           中於任何單一投資項目或單一類型產品，確
investment types and investment targets, and tiered management,          保投資策略與本公司發展戰略一致。
the Group seeks to prioritize the safety of the Company’s funds,
mitigate the risk of investment losses, avoid over-concentration in
any single investment project or single type of product, and align its
investment strategies with the Company’s development strategy.

The following is a summary of the principal terms of the Pre-IPO        以下為經2021年6月1日（「首次公開發售前
RSU Scheme approved and adopted pursuant to a resolution of             採納日期」）董事會決議案批准及採納並於
the Board dated June 1, 2021 (the “Pre-IPO Adoption Date”) and          2023年2月20日修訂的首次公開發售前受限
amended on February 20, 2023.                                           制股份單位計劃的主要條款概要。

The Pre-IPO RSU Scheme is not subject to the provisions of              首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃毋須遵
Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules as it does not involve any grant        守上市規則第十七章條文的規定，原因是其
of share options or awards by the Company after the Listing.            並不涉及本公司於上市後任何授出購股權或
Accordingly, (i) as at the beginning and end of the Reporting Period,   獎勵的行為。因此，(i)於報告期初及期末，
no Shares underlying the Awards are available for grants under the      概無任何獎勵所涉及的股份可供根據首次公
Pre-IPO RSU Scheme; (ii) as at the date of this interim report, no      開發售前受限制股份單位計劃授出；(ii)於本
share is available for issue under the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme.              中期報告日期，概無任何股份可供根據首次
                                                                        公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃發行。

(1)   Purpose                                                           (1)    目的
      The purpose of the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme is to reward the                   首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃的
      grantees for their services and contribution to the success              目的乃為嘉許承授人對本集團的成功作
      of the Group, and to provide incentives to them to further               出服務及貢獻，並為彼等對本集團的進
      contribute to the Group.                                                 一步貢獻提供獎勵。

(2)   RSU Awards                                                        (2)    受限制股份單位獎勵
      An award of RSUs under the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme                            首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃項
      (“Award(s)”) gives the participant a contingent right upon               下的受限制股份單位獎勵（「獎勵」）給
      vesting of the Award to obtain either Shares or an equivalent            予參與者一項或有權利，在獎勵歸屬時
      value in cash with reference to the market value of the Shares           獲取經董事會參考歸屬日期或前後有關
      underlying such Award on or about the date of vesting, as                獎勵涉及的股份的市值而全權酌情釐定
      determined by the Board in its sole discretion.                          的股份或等值現金。

(3)   Participants                                                      (3)    參與者
      Persons eligible to receive RSUs under the Pre-IPO RSU                   合資格獲取首次公開發售前受限制股份
      Scheme are any director, full-time or part-time employee of              單位計劃項下受限制股份單位的人士為
      the Group who the Board considers, in its sole discretion, has           董事會全權酌情釐定認為已經或將會對
      contributed or will contribute to the Group (the “RSU Eligible           本集團作出貢獻的本集團任何董事、全
      Person(s)”). The Board selects the RSU Eligible Persons                  職或兼職僱員（「受限制股份單位合資
      to receive Awards under the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme at its                    格人士」）。董事會酌情選定獲取首次公
      discretion.                                                              開發售前受限制股份單位計劃項下獎勵
                                                                               的受限制股份單位合資格人士。

Incentive Scheme approved pursuant to a resolution of the Board          期」）董事會決議案批准的2024年股份激勵計
dated September 27, 2024 (the “2024 Adoption Date”). The 2024            劃的主要條款概要。2024年股份激勵計劃僅
Share Incentive Scheme shall be funded solely by existing Shares         以現有股份撥資，並須遵守上市規則第17.12
and shall be subject to applicable requirements under Rule 17.12 of      條項下的適用規定。因此，於本中期報告日
the Listing Rules. Accordingly, as at the date of this interim report,   期，概無任何股份可供根據2024年股份激勵
no shares are available for issue under the 2024 Share Incentive         計劃發行。
Scheme.

(1)   Purpose                                                            (1)    目的
      The purpose of the 2024 Share Incentive Scheme is to reward               2024年股份激勵計劃的目的乃為嘉許
      the participants for their services and contribution to the               參與者對本集團的成功作出服務及貢
      success of the Group, and to provide incentives to them to                獻，並激勵彼等進一步為本集團作出貢
      further contribute to the Group.                                          獻。

      Share Incentive Scheme (“Award(s)”) gives the participant a               份單位（「受限制股份單位」）獎勵（「獎

      Shares or an equivalent value in cash with reference to the               勵歸屬時獲取由董事會參考歸屬日期或
      market value of the Shares underlying such Award on or                    前後有關獎勵涉及的股份的市值而全權
      about the date of vesting, as determined by the Board at its              酌情釐定的股份或等值現金。
      sole discretion.

      discretion, has contributed or will contribute to the Group (the          獻的本集團任何董事、全職或兼職僱員

2025 SHARE INCENTIVE SCHEME                                                   2025年股份激勵計劃
The following is a summary of the principal terms of the 2025 Share           以下為經2025年8月28日董事會決議案批准
Incentive Scheme as approved by a resolution of the Board dated               及2025年10月27日（「2025年採納日期」）股
August 28, 2025 and passed by a resolution of the extraordinary               東特別大會決議案通過的2025年股份激勵計
general meeting dated October 27, 2025 (the “2025 Adoption                    劃的主要條款概要。2025年股份激勵計劃由
Date”). The 2025 Share Incentive Scheme is funded by the issue                發行新股份（包括轉讓庫存股份）撥資，並須
of new Shares, including the transfer of treasury Shares, and shall           遵守上市規則第17章項下的適用規定。
be subject to the applicable requirements under Chapter 17 of the
Listing Rules.

(1)   Purpose                                                                 (1)    目的
      The purpose of the 2025 Share Incentive Scheme is to attract                   2025年股份激勵計劃旨在吸引並挽留
      and retain outstanding talents, to reward the selected persons                 傑出人才以及嘉許經選定人士為本集團
      for their services and contributions to the success of the                     的成功提供的服務及作出的貢獻，並激
      Group, and to provide incentives to them to further contribute                 勵彼等繼續為本集團作出貢獻。
      to the Group.

(2)   Awards                                                                  (2)    獎勵
      An award of restricted share units (“RSUs”) under the 2025                     2025年股份激勵計劃項下的受限制股
      Share Incentive Scheme (“Award(s)”) gives the participant a                    份單位（「受限制股份單位」）獎勵（「獎
      contingent right upon vesting of the Award to obtain either                    勵」）給予參與者一項或有權利，可於獎
      Shares or an equivalent value in cash with reference to the                    勵歸屬時獲取由董事會參考歸屬日期或
      market value of the Shares underlying such Award on or                         前後有關獎勵涉及的股份的市值而全權
      about the date of vesting, as determined by the Board at                       酌情釐定的股份或等值現金。當本公司
      its sole discretion. When the Company grants Awards to a                       向承授人授予獎勵時，承授人將被視為
      grantee, the grantee will be deemed to have accepted the                       已接納該等獎勵。承授人於接納該等獎
      Awards. The grantee shall not be required to pay any amount                    勵時毋須支付任何款項。
      upon acceptance of the Awards.

      An award of options under the 2025 Share Incentive Scheme                      根據2025年股份激勵計劃授出的購股
      gives the participant the right to subscribe for a specified                   權獎勵，賦予參與者按行使價認購指定
      number of Shares at the exercise price.                                        數目股份的權利。

               The level into which a fair value measurement                         值計量）進行分類。劃分公
               is classified is determined with reference to the                     平值計量級別時，乃參照

                    only Level 1 inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted                              第 一 級 輸 入 值（即 相

                                                                                            未 經 調 整 報 價 ）計 量
                                                                                            的公平值


               •    Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using                    •      第二級估值：使用第
                    Level 2 inputs i.e. observable inputs which                             二 級 輸 入 值（即 未 能
                    fail to meet Level 1, and not using significant                         符合第一級的可觀察
                    unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs                                輸 入 值 ）， 而 不 使 用
                    are inputs for which market data are not                                重大不可觀察輸入值
                    available                                                               計量的公平值。不可
                                                                                            觀察輸入值為缺乏市
                                                                                            場數據的輸入值


               •    Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using                    •      第三級估值：使用重
                    significant unobservable inputs                                         大不可觀察輸入值計
                                                                                            量的公平值

                                               investment advisors. It provides dedicated investment advisory
                                               services and is equipped with simulated portfolios, video live streams,
                                               expert courses, software indicators and article columns, and is
                                               designed to help investors induce right investing concepts, build a
                                               scientific and systematic investment system and enhance investment
                                               satisfaction
「超級投資家」                             指          由九方金融研究所匯集眾多資深投資顧問打造的投資教學類服務產品，
                                               提供專屬投資顧問服務、配備模擬組合、視頻直播、大咖課程、軟件指
                                               標、文章專欄等，助力投資者構築正確的投資理念，構築科學系統的投
                                               資體系，提升投資幸福感

“traffic”                                      in terms of traffic in our market matrix, the flow of audience on various
                                               media platforms
「流量」                                指          就我們市場矩陣中的流量而言，為多個媒體平台的受眾流量

“UV”                                          acronym for “unique visitor”, a distinct individual user who accesses
                                              a website, App, mini-program or online platform via terminal devices
                                              within a specific statistical period
